+struct LocalValidation {
+    int finite;
+    double minValue;
+    double maxValue;
+};
+
+LocalValidation validateLocal(const double* const c, const size_t localNz, const size_t nx,
+                              const size_t ny, const size_t rowStride,
+                              const size_t planeStride) {
+    LocalValidation result{1, std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity(),
+                           -std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity()};
+    for (size_t z = 1; z <= localNz; ++z) {
+        const double* const plane = c + z * planeStride;
+        for (size_t y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            const double* const row = plane + y * rowStride;
+            for (size_t x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const double value = row[x];
+                if (!std::isfinite(value)) {
+                    result.finite = 0;
+                    return result;
+                }
+                result.minValue = std::min(result.minValue, value);
+                result.maxValue = std::max(result.maxValue, value);
             }
         }
     }
+    return result;

    22	
    23	// Each physical plane has a one-cell X/Y halo.  This makes all stencil reads
    24	// branch-free; the Z halos are exchanged with adjacent MPI ranks.
    25	void fillLateralHalos(double* const field, const size_t localNz, const size_t nx,
    26	                      const size_t ny, const size_t rowStride, const size_t planeStride) {
    27	    for (size_t z = 1; z <= localNz; ++z) {
    28	        double* const plane = field + z * planeStride;
    29	        for (size_t y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
    30	            double* const row = plane + y * rowStride;
    31	            row[0] = row[1];
    32	            row[nx + 1] = row[nx];
    33	        }
    34	        std::copy_n(plane + rowStride, rowStride, plane);
    35	        std::copy_n(plane + ny * rowStride, rowStride, plane + (ny + 1) * rowStride);
    36	    }
    37	}
    38	
    39	struct HaloExchange {
    40	    MPI_Request requests[4];
    41	    int count = 0;
    42	};
    43	
    44	HaloExchange startZHaloExchange(double* const field, const size_t localNz,
    45	                                const size_t planeStride, const int rank,
    46	                                const int ranks, const MPI_Comm comm) {
    47	    constexpr int upperPlaneTag = 701;
    48	    constexpr int lowerPlaneTag = 702;
    49	    const int planeCount = static_cast<int>(planeStride);
    50	    HaloExchange exchange;
    51	
    52	    if (rank == 0) {
    53	        std::copy_n(field + planeStride, planeStride, field);
    54	    } else {
    55	        MPI_Irecv(field, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, rank - 1, upperPlaneTag, comm,
    56	                  &exchange.requests[exchange.count++]);
    57	    }
    58	    if (rank == ranks - 1) {
    59	        std::copy_n(field + localNz * planeStride, planeStride,
    60	                    field + (localNz + 1) * planeStride);
    61	    } else {
    62	        MPI_Irecv(field + (localNz + 1) * planeStride, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
    63	                  rank + 1, lowerPlaneTag, comm, &exchange.requests[exchange.count++]);
    64	    }
    65	    if (rank != 0) {
    66	        MPI_Isend(field + planeStride, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, rank - 1, lowerPlaneTag, comm,
    67	                  &exchange.requests[exchange.count++]);
    68	    }
    69	    if (rank != ranks - 1) {
    70	        MPI_Isend(field + localNz * planeStride, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, upperPlaneTag,
    71	                  comm, &exchange.requests[exchange.count++]);
    72	    }
    73	    return exchange;
    74	}
    75	
    76	void finishHaloExchange(HaloExchange& exchange) {
    77	    if (exchange.count != 0) {
    78	        MPI_Waitall(exchange.count, exchange.requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
    79	    }
    80	}
    81	
    82	void computeChemicalPotentialRange(const double* CH_RESTRICT const c,
    83	                                   double* CH_RESTRICT const mu, const size_t firstZ,
    84	                                   const size_t lastZ, const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
    85	                                   const size_t rowStride, const size_t planeStride,
    86	                                   const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
    87	                                   const double gamma, const double eAA, const double eBB,
    88	                                   const double eAB) {
    89	    for (size_t z = firstZ; z <= lastZ; ++z) {
    90	        const double* const previousPlane = c + (z - 1) * planeStride;
    91	        const double* const currentPlane = c + z * planeStride;
    92	        const double* const nextPlane = c + (z + 1) * planeStride;
    93	        double* const outputPlane = mu + z * planeStride;
    94	
    95	        for (size_t y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
    96	            const double* const previousRow = currentPlane + (y - 1) * rowStride;
    97	            const double* const currentRow = currentPlane + y * rowStride;
    98	            const double* const nextRow = currentPlane + (y + 1) * rowStride;
    99	            double* const outputRow = outputPlane + y * rowStride;
   100	            const double* const previousZRow = previousPlane + y * rowStride;
   101	            const double* const nextZRow = nextPlane + y * rowStride;
   102	
   103	            for (size_t x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
   104	                const double cv = currentRow[x];
   105	                const double cxx = (currentRow[x + 1] + currentRow[x - 1] - 2.0 * cv) / (dx * dx);
   106	                const double cyy = (nextRow[x] + previousRow[x] - 2.0 * cv) / (dy * dy);
   107	                const double czz = (nextZRow[x] + previousZRow[x] - 2.0 * cv) / (dz * dz);
   108	                outputRow[x] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB - 2.0 * cv * eAB) +
   109	                               3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);
   110	            }
   111	        }
   112	    }
   113	}
   114	
   115	void cahnHilliardUpdateRange(double* CH_RESTRICT const cnew, const double* CH_RESTRICT const cold,
   116	                             const double* CH_RESTRICT const mu, const size_t firstZ,
   117	                             const size_t lastZ, const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
   118	                             const size_t rowStride, const size_t planeStride, const double D,
   119	                             const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
   120	    for (size_t z = firstZ; z <= lastZ; ++z) {
   121	        const double* const previousPlane = mu + (z - 1) * planeStride;
   122	        const double* const currentPlane = mu + z * planeStride;
   123	        const double* const nextPlane = mu + (z + 1) * planeStride;
   124	        const double* const oldPlane = cold + z * planeStride;
   125	        double* const newPlane = cnew + z * planeStride;
   126	
   127	        for (size_t y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
   128	            const double* const previousRow = currentPlane + (y - 1) * rowStride;
   129	            const double* const currentRow = currentPlane + y * rowStride;
   130	            const double* const nextRow = currentPlane + (y + 1) * rowStride;
   131	            const double* const oldRow = oldPlane + y * rowStride;
   132	            double* const newRow = newPlane + y * rowStride;
   133	            const double* const previousZRow = previousPlane + y * rowStride;
   134	            const double* const nextZRow = nextPlane + y * rowStride;
   135	
   136	            for (size_t x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
   137	                const double muv = currentRow[x];
   138	                const double mxx = (currentRow[x + 1] + currentRow[x - 1] - 2.0 * muv) / (dx * dx);
   139	                const double myy = (nextRow[x] + previousRow[x] - 2.0 * muv) / (dy * dy);
   140	                const double mzz = (nextZRow[x] + previousZRow[x] - 2.0 * muv) / (dz * dz);
   141	                newRow[x] = oldRow[x] + dt * D * (mxx + myy + mzz);
   142	            }
   143	        }
   144	    }
   145	}
   146	
   147	void initializeConcentration(double* const c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
   148	                             const size_t localNz, const size_t globalZStart,
   149	                             const size_t rowStride, const size_t planeStride,
   150	                             const size_t globalVolume) {
   151	    const size_t xySize = nx * ny;
   152	    for (size_t localZ = 0; localZ < localNz; ++localZ) {
   153	        const size_t globalZ = globalZStart + localZ;
   154	        double* const plane = c + (localZ + 1) * planeStride;
   155	        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
   156	            double* const row = plane + (y + 1) * rowStride + 1;
   157	            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
   158	                const size_t linearId = globalZ * xySize + y * nx + x;
   159	                const double pseudo = ((((linearId + 1) * 1299709) % globalVolume) /
   160	                                       static_cast<double>(globalVolume));
   161	                row[x] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
   162	            }
   163	        }
   164	    }
   165	}
   166	
   167	struct LocalValidation {
   168	    int finite;
   169	    double minValue;
   170	    double maxValue;
   171	};
   172	
   173	LocalValidation validateLocal(const double* const c, const size_t localNz, const size_t nx,
   174	                              const size_t ny, const size_t rowStride,
   175	                              const size_t planeStride) {
   176	    LocalValidation result{1, std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity(),
   177	                           -std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity()};
   178	    for (size_t z = 1; z <= localNz; ++z) {
   179	        const double* const plane = c + z * planeStride;
   180	        for (size_t y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
   181	            const double* const row = plane + y * rowStride;
   182	            for (size_t x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
   183	                const double value = row[x];
   184	                if (!std::isfinite(value)) {
   185	                    result.finite = 0;
   186	                    return result;
   187	                }
   188	                result.minValue = std::min(result.minValue, value);
   189	                result.maxValue = std::max(result.maxValue, value);
   190	            }
   191	        }
   192	    }
   193	    return result;
   194	}
   195	
   196	void packInterior(const double* const field, std::vector<double>& packed, const size_t localNz,
   197	                  const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t rowStride,
   198	                  const size_t planeStride) {
   199	    size_t destination = 0;
   200	    for (size_t z = 1; z <= localNz; ++z) {
   201	        const double* const plane = field + z * planeStride;
   202	        for (size_t y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
   203	            std::copy_n(plane + y * rowStride + 1, nx, packed.data() + destination);
   204	            destination += nx;
   205	        }
   206	    }
   207	}
   208	
   209	bool multiplyWouldOverflow(const size_t left, const size_t right) {
   210	    return left != 0 && right > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / left;
   211	}
   212	
   213	bool parseSize(const char* const text, size_t& value) {
   214	    errno = 0;
   215	    char* end = nullptr;
   216	    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
   217	    if (errno == ERANGE || end == text || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
   218	        parsed > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
   219	        return false;
   220	    }
   221	    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
   222	    return true;
   223	}
   224	
   225	bool parseIterations(const char* const text, int& value) {
   226	    errno = 0;
   227	    char* end = nullptr;
   228	    const long parsed = std::strtol(text, &end, 10);
   229	    if (errno == ERANGE || end == text || *end != '\0' || parsed < 0 || parsed > INT_MAX) {
   230	        return false;
   231	    }
   232	    value = static_cast<int>(parsed);
   233	    return true;
   234	}

   236	}  // namespace

   238	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   239	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   240	    printf("Options:\n");
   241	    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
   242	    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
   243	    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
   244	    printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
   245	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   246	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   247	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   248	}
   249	
   250	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   251	    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
   252	
   253	    int worldRank = 0;
   254	    int worldSize = 0;
   255	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldRank);
   256	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);
   257	
   258	    struct Configuration {
   259	        size_t nx = 64;
   260	        size_t ny = 0;
   261	        size_t nz = 0;
   262	        int iterations = 20;
   263	        int validate = 0;
   264	        int printResults = 0;
   265	        int status = 0;  // 0: run, 1: help, 2: invalid arguments
   266	    } configuration;
   267	
   268	    if (worldRank == 0) {
   269	        for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   270	            if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   271	                if (!parseSize(argv[++i], configuration.nx)) configuration.status = 2;
   272	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   273	                if (!parseSize(argv[++i], configuration.ny)) configuration.status = 2;
   274	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   275	                if (!parseSize(argv[++i], configuration.nz)) configuration.status = 2;
   276	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   277	                if (!parseIterations(argv[++i], configuration.iterations)) configuration.status = 2;
   278	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   279	                configuration.validate = 1;
   280	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   281	                configuration.printResults = 1;
   282	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   283	                configuration.status = 1;
   284	            } else {
   285	                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   286	                configuration.status = 2;
   287	            }
   288	        }
   289	        if (configuration.status == 1 || configuration.status == 2) {
   290	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   291	        }
   292	    }
   293	
   294	    MPI_Bcast(&configuration, static_cast<int>(sizeof(configuration)), MPI_BYTE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   295	    if (configuration.status != 0) {
   296	        MPI_Finalize();
   297	        return configuration.status == 1 ? 0 : 1;
   298	    }
   299	
   300	    if (configuration.ny == 0) configuration.ny = configuration.nx;
   301	    if (configuration.nz == 0) configuration.nz = configuration.nx;
   302	    const size_t nx = configuration.nx;
   303	    const size_t ny = configuration.ny;
   304	    const size_t nz = configuration.nz;
   305	    const int iterations = configuration.iterations;
   306	
   307	    if (multiplyWouldOverflow(nx, ny) || multiplyWouldOverflow(nx * ny, nz) ||
   308	        nx > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() - 2 ||
   309	        ny > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() - 2 ||
   310	        multiplyWouldOverflow(nx + 2, ny + 2)) {
   311	        if (worldRank == 0) printf("Invalid grid size: dimensions are too large\n");
   312	        MPI_Finalize();
   313	        return 1;
   314	    }
   315	
   316	    const size_t physicalPlaneSize = nx * ny;
   317	    const size_t gridSize = physicalPlaneSize * nz;
   318	    const size_t rowStride = nx + 2;
   319	    const size_t planeStride = rowStride * (ny + 2);
   320	    if (planeStride > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) ||
   321	        (configuration.printResults && gridSize > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX))) {
   322	        if (worldRank == 0) printf("Grid size exceeds the supported MPI message count\n");
   323	        MPI_Finalize();
   324	        return 1;
   325	    }
   326	
   327	    const int activeRanks = static_cast<int>(std::min(nz, static_cast<size_t>(worldSize)));
   328	    MPI_Comm activeComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
   329	    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, worldRank < activeRanks ? 0 : MPI_UNDEFINED, worldRank, &activeComm);
   330	    if (worldRank >= activeRanks) {
   331	        MPI_Finalize();
   332	        return 0;
   333	    }
   334	
   335	    int rank = 0;
   336	    int ranks = 0;
   337	    MPI_Comm_rank(activeComm, &rank);
   338	    MPI_Comm_size(activeComm, &ranks);
   339	    const size_t basePlanes = nz / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
   340	    const size_t remainderPlanes = nz % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
   341	    const size_t localNz = basePlanes + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainderPlanes ? 1 : 0);
   342	    const size_t globalZStart = static_cast<size_t>(rank) * basePlanes +
   343	                                std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), remainderPlanes);
   344	    if (multiplyWouldOverflow(localNz + 2, planeStride)) {
   345	        if (rank == 0) printf("Grid size exceeds local addressable memory\n");
   346	        MPI_Comm_free(&activeComm);
   347	        MPI_Finalize();
   348	        return 1;
   349	    }
   350	
   351	    if (rank == 0) {
   352	        printf("Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\n");
   353	        printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
   354	        printf("Time steps: %d\n", iterations);
   355	        printf("Validation: %s\n", configuration.validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   356	    }
   357	
   358	    const double dx = 1.0;
   359	    const double dy = 1.0;
   360	    const double dz = 1.0;
   361	    const double dt = 0.01;
   362	    const double eAA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
   363	    const double eBB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
   364	    const double eAB = 2.0 / 9.0;
   365	    const double gamma = 0.5;
   366	    const double D = 1.0;
   367	
   368	    const size_t localStorageSize = (localNz + 2) * planeStride;
   369	    std::vector<double> cold(localStorageSize);
   370	    std::vector<double> cnew(localStorageSize);
   371	    std::vector<double> mu(localStorageSize);
   372	
   373	    if (rank == 0) printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
   374	    initializeConcentration(cold.data(), nx, ny, localNz, globalZStart, rowStride, planeStride, gridSize);
   375	    fillLateralHalos(cold.data(), localNz, nx, ny, rowStride, planeStride);
   376	
   377	    if (rank == 0) printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
   378	    MPI_Barrier(activeComm);
   379	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   380	    for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
   381	        HaloExchange coldExchange = startZHaloExchange(cold.data(), localNz, planeStride, rank, ranks, activeComm);
   382	        if (localNz > 2) {
   383	            computeChemicalPotentialRange(cold.data(), mu.data(), 2, localNz - 1, nx, ny, rowStride,
   384	                                          planeStride, dx, dy, dz, gamma, eAA, eBB, eAB);
   385	        }
   386	        finishHaloExchange(coldExchange);
   387	        computeChemicalPotentialRange(cold.data(), mu.data(), 1, 1, nx, ny, rowStride, planeStride,
   388	                                      dx, dy, dz, gamma, eAA, eBB, eAB);
   389	        if (localNz > 1) {
   390	            computeChemicalPotentialRange(cold.data(), mu.data(), localNz, localNz, nx, ny, rowStride,
   391	                                          planeStride, dx, dy, dz, gamma, eAA, eBB, eAB);
   392	        }
   393	        fillLateralHalos(mu.data(), localNz, nx, ny, rowStride, planeStride);
   394	
   395	        HaloExchange muExchange = startZHaloExchange(mu.data(), localNz, planeStride, rank, ranks, activeComm);
   396	        if (localNz > 2) {
   397	            cahnHilliardUpdateRange(cnew.data(), cold.data(), mu.data(), 2, localNz - 1, nx, ny,
   398	                                    rowStride, planeStride, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);
   399	        }
   400	        finishHaloExchange(muExchange);
   401	        cahnHilliardUpdateRange(cnew.data(), cold.data(), mu.data(), 1, 1, nx, ny, rowStride,
   402	                                planeStride, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);
   403	        if (localNz > 1) {
   404	            cahnHilliardUpdateRange(cnew.data(), cold.data(), mu.data(), localNz, localNz, nx, ny,
   405	                                    rowStride, planeStride, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);
   406	        }
   407	        fillLateralHalos(cnew.data(), localNz, nx, ny, rowStride, planeStride);
   408	        std::swap(cold, cnew);
   409	    }
   410	
   411	    const double localDuration = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   412	    double duration = 0.0;
   413	    MPI_Reduce(&localDuration, &duration, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, activeComm);
   414	    if (rank == 0) {
   415	        printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", static_cast<long>(duration * 1000.0));
   416	        const double cellUpdates = static_cast<double>(gridSize) * iterations;
   417	        const double mcups = duration > 0.0 ? cellUpdates / duration / 1e6 : 0.0;
   418	        printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
   419	    }
   420	
   421	    if (configuration.printResults) {
   422	        const size_t localElementCount = localNz * physicalPlaneSize;
   423	        std::vector<double> packed(localElementCount);
   424	        packInterior(cold.data(), packed, localNz, nx, ny, rowStride, planeStride);
   425	
   426	        std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
   427	        std::vector<int> displacements;
   428	        std::vector<double> globalResult;
   429	        if (rank == 0) {
   430	            receiveCounts.resize(ranks);
   431	            displacements.resize(ranks);
   432	            globalResult.resize(gridSize);
   433	            for (int process = 0; process < ranks; ++process) {
   434	                const size_t processPlanes = basePlanes +
   435	                    (static_cast<size_t>(process) < remainderPlanes ? 1 : 0);
   436	                const size_t processStart = static_cast<size_t>(process) * basePlanes +
   437	                    std::min(static_cast<size_t>(process), remainderPlanes);
   438	                receiveCounts[process] = static_cast<int>(processPlanes * physicalPlaneSize);
   439	                displacements[process] = static_cast<int>(processStart * physicalPlaneSize);
   440	            }
   441	        }
   442	        MPI_Gatherv(packed.data(), static_cast<int>(localElementCount), MPI_DOUBLE,
   443	                    rank == 0 ? globalResult.data() : nullptr,
   444	                    rank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
   445	                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, activeComm);
   446	        if (rank == 0) print_results(globalResult, "Concentration");
   447	    }
   448	
   449	    int returnCode = 0;
   450	    if (configuration.validate) {
   451	        if (rank == 0) printf("Validating result...\n");
   452	        const LocalValidation localValidation =
   453	            validateLocal(cold.data(), localNz, nx, ny, rowStride, planeStride);
   454	        int allFinite = 0;
   455	        double minValue = 0.0;
   456	        double maxValue = 0.0;
   457	        MPI_Allreduce(&localValidation.finite, &allFinite, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, activeComm);
   458	        MPI_Reduce(&localValidation.minValue, &minValue, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MIN, 0, activeComm);
   459	        MPI_Reduce(&localValidation.maxValue, &maxValue, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, activeComm);
   460	        if (rank == 0) {
   461	            if (!allFinite) {
   462	                printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
   463	                printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   464	                returnCode = 1;
   465	            } else {
   466	                printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minValue, maxValue);
   467	                if (maxValue > 10.0 || minValue < -10.0) {
   468	                    printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
   469	                    printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   470	                    returnCode = 1;
   471	                } else {
   472	                    printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   473	                }
   474	            }
   475	        }
   476	        MPI_Bcast(&returnCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, activeComm);
   477	    }
   478	
   479	    MPI_Comm_free(&activeComm);
   480	    MPI_Finalize();
   481	    return returnCode;
   482	}

-inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {

+namespace {
+
+#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
+#define CH_RESTRICT __restrict__
+#else
+#define CH_RESTRICT

+// Each physical plane has a one-cell X/Y halo.  This makes all stencil reads
+// branch-free; the Z halos are exchanged with adjacent MPI ranks.
+void fillLateralHalos(double* const field, const size_t localNz, const size_t nx,
+                      const size_t ny, const size_t rowStride, const size_t planeStride) {
+    for (size_t z = 1; z <= localNz; ++z) {
+        double* const plane = field + z * planeStride;
+        for (size_t y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            double* const row = plane + y * rowStride;
+            row[0] = row[1];
+            row[nx + 1] = row[nx];
+        }
+        std::copy_n(plane + rowStride, rowStride, plane);
+        std::copy_n(plane + ny * rowStride, rowStride, plane + (ny + 1) * rowStride);
+    }
 }

+struct HaloExchange {
+    MPI_Request requests[4];
+    int count = 0;
+};
+
+HaloExchange startZHaloExchange(double* const field, const size_t localNz,
+                                const size_t planeStride, const int rank,
+                                const int ranks, const MPI_Comm comm) {
+    constexpr int upperPlaneTag = 701;
+    constexpr int lowerPlaneTag = 702;
+    const int planeCount = static_cast<int>(planeStride);
+    HaloExchange exchange;
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::copy_n(field + planeStride, planeStride, field);
+    } else {
+        MPI_Irecv(field, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, rank - 1, upperPlaneTag, comm,
+                  &exchange.requests[exchange.count++]);
+    }
+    if (rank == ranks - 1) {
+        std::copy_n(field + localNz * planeStride, planeStride,
+                    field + (localNz + 1) * planeStride);
+    } else {
+        MPI_Irecv(field + (localNz + 1) * planeStride, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  rank + 1, lowerPlaneTag, comm, &exchange.requests[exchange.count++]);
+    }
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        MPI_Isend(field + planeStride, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, rank - 1, lowerPlaneTag, comm,
+                  &exchange.requests[exchange.count++]);
+    }
+    if (rank != ranks - 1) {
+        MPI_Isend(field + localNz * planeStride, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, upperPlaneTag,
+                  comm, &exchange.requests[exchange.count++]);
+    }
+    return exchange;
 }

+void finishHaloExchange(HaloExchange& exchange) {
+    if (exchange.count != 0) {
+        MPI_Waitall(exchange.count, exchange.requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+    }
+}

+void computeChemicalPotentialRange(const double* CH_RESTRICT const c,
+                                   double* CH_RESTRICT const mu, const size_t firstZ,
+                                   const size_t lastZ, const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                                   const size_t rowStride, const size_t planeStride,
+                                   const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                                   const double gamma, const double eAA, const double eBB,
+                                   const double eAB) {
+    for (size_t z = firstZ; z <= lastZ; ++z) {
+        const double* const previousPlane = c + (z - 1) * planeStride;
+        const double* const currentPlane = c + z * planeStride;
+        const double* const nextPlane = c + (z + 1) * planeStride;
+        double* const outputPlane = mu + z * planeStride;
+
+        for (size_t y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            const double* const previousRow = currentPlane + (y - 1) * rowStride;
+            const double* const currentRow = currentPlane + y * rowStride;
+            const double* const nextRow = currentPlane + (y + 1) * rowStride;
+            double* const outputRow = outputPlane + y * rowStride;
+            const double* const previousZRow = previousPlane + y * rowStride;
+            const double* const nextZRow = nextPlane + y * rowStride;
+
+            for (size_t x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const double cv = currentRow[x];
+                const double cxx = (currentRow[x + 1] + currentRow[x - 1] - 2.0 * cv) / (dx * dx);
+                const double cyy = (nextRow[x] + previousRow[x] - 2.0 * cv) / (dy * dy);
+                const double czz = (nextZRow[x] + previousZRow[x] - 2.0 * cv) / (dz * dz);
+                outputRow[x] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB - 2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                               3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);
             }
         }
     }
 }

+void cahnHilliardUpdateRange(double* CH_RESTRICT const cnew, const double* CH_RESTRICT const cold,
+                             const double* CH_RESTRICT const mu, const size_t firstZ,
+                             const size_t lastZ, const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                             const size_t rowStride, const size_t planeStride, const double D,
+                             const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
+    for (size_t z = firstZ; z <= lastZ; ++z) {
+        const double* const previousPlane = mu + (z - 1) * planeStride;
+        const double* const currentPlane = mu + z * planeStride;
+        const double* const nextPlane = mu + (z + 1) * planeStride;
+        const double* const oldPlane = cold + z * planeStride;
+        double* const newPlane = cnew + z * planeStride;
+
+        for (size_t y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            const double* const previousRow = currentPlane + (y - 1) * rowStride;
+            const double* const currentRow = currentPlane + y * rowStride;
+            const double* const nextRow = currentPlane + (y + 1) * rowStride;
+            const double* const oldRow = oldPlane + y * rowStride;
+            double* const newRow = newPlane + y * rowStride;
+            const double* const previousZRow = previousPlane + y * rowStride;
+            const double* const nextZRow = nextPlane + y * rowStride;
+
+            for (size_t x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const double muv = currentRow[x];
+                const double mxx = (currentRow[x + 1] + currentRow[x - 1] - 2.0 * muv) / (dx * dx);
+                const double myy = (nextRow[x] + previousRow[x] - 2.0 * muv) / (dy * dy);

+                newRow[x] = oldRow[x] + dt * D * (mxx + myy + mzz);
             }
         }
     }

codex
Implemented MPI distributed-memory parallelism.

- Z-slab decomposition with nonblocking halo exchanges overlapped with interior stencil computation.
- Branch-free X/Y stencil halos and optimized local storage.
- Root-only output, distributed validation, and gathered `-r` results preserve serial semantics.
- MPI is now required in [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/cahn-hilliard_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_mpi_r3/cahn-hilliard/CMakeLists.txt:7); executable name remains `cahn_hilliard`.
- Main implementation: [cahn_hilliard.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/cahn-hilliard_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_mpi_r3/cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp:44).

Validated a Release build and exact `-r` output agreement between 1, 2, 3, and excess-rank MPI runs, including single-cell/thin-domain cases.

-inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {

+namespace {
+
+#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
+#define CH_RESTRICT __restrict__
+#else
+#define CH_RESTRICT
+#endif
+
+// Each physical plane has a one-cell X/Y halo.  This makes all stencil reads
+// branch-free; the Z halos are exchanged with adjacent MPI ranks.
+void fillLateralHalos(double* const field, const size_t localNz, const size_t nx,
+                      const size_t ny, const size_t rowStride, const size_t planeStride) {
+    for (size_t z = 1; z <= localNz; ++z) {
+        double* const plane = field + z * planeStride;
+        for (size_t y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            double* const row = plane + y * rowStride;
+            row[0] = row[1];
+            row[nx + 1] = row[nx];
+        }
+        std::copy_n(plane + rowStride, rowStride, plane);
+        std::copy_n(plane + ny * rowStride, rowStride, plane + (ny + 1) * rowStride);
+    }
 }
 
-// Compute Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions
-double computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {
-    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;
-    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;
-    const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;
-    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;
-    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;
-    const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;
-    
-    const double cxx = (c[idx3(xp, y, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(xn, y, z, nx, ny)] - 
-                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dx * dx);
-    const double cyy = (c[idx3(x, yp, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, yn, z, nx, ny)] - 
-                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dy * dy);
-    const double czz = (c[idx3(x, y, zp, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, y, zn, nx, ny)] -

-    
-    return cxx + cyy + czz;
+struct HaloExchange {
+    MPI_Request requests[4];
+    int count = 0;
+};
+
+HaloExchange startZHaloExchange(double* const field, const size_t localNz,
+                                const size_t planeStride, const int rank,
+                                const int ranks, const MPI_Comm comm) {
+    constexpr int upperPlaneTag = 701;
+    constexpr int lowerPlaneTag = 702;
+    const int planeCount = static_cast<int>(planeStride);
+    HaloExchange exchange;
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::copy_n(field + planeStride, planeStride, field);
+    } else {
+        MPI_Irecv(field, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, rank - 1, upperPlaneTag, comm,
+                  &exchange.requests[exchange.count++]);
+    }
+    if (rank == ranks - 1) {
+        std::copy_n(field + localNz * planeStride, planeStride,
+                    field + (localNz + 1) * planeStride);
+    } else {
+        MPI_Irecv(field + (localNz + 1) * planeStride, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  rank + 1, lowerPlaneTag, comm, &exchange.requests[exchange.count++]);
+    }
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        MPI_Isend(field + planeStride, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, rank - 1, lowerPlaneTag, comm,
+                  &exchange.requests[exchange.count++]);
+    }
+    if (rank != ranks - 1) {
+        MPI_Isend(field + localNz * planeStride, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, upperPlaneTag,
+                  comm, &exchange.requests[exchange.count++]);
+    }
+    return exchange;
 }
 
-// Compute chemical potential
-void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
-                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
-                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                const double cv = c[idx];
-                
-                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) 
-                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
-                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+void finishHaloExchange(HaloExchange& exchange) {
+    if (exchange.count != 0) {
+        MPI_Waitall(exchange.count, exchange.requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+    }
+}
+
+void computeChemicalPotentialRange(const double* CH_RESTRICT const c,
+                                   double* CH_RESTRICT const mu, const size_t firstZ,
+                                   const size_t lastZ, const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                                   const size_t rowStride, const size_t planeStride,
+                                   const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                                   const double gamma, const double eAA, const double eBB,
+                                   const double eAB) {
+    for (size_t z = firstZ; z <= lastZ; ++z) {
+        const double* const previousPlane = c + (z - 1) * planeStride;
+        const double* const currentPlane = c + z * planeStride;
+        const double* const nextPlane = c + (z + 1) * planeStride;
+        double* const outputPlane = mu + z * planeStride;
+
+        for (size_t y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            const double* const previousRow = currentPlane + (y - 1) * rowStride;
+            const double* const currentRow = currentPlane + y * rowStride;
+            const double* const nextRow = currentPlane + (y + 1) * rowStride;
+            double* const outputRow = outputPlane + y * rowStride;
+            const double* const previousZRow = previousPlane + y * rowStride;
+            const double* const nextZRow = nextPlane + y * rowStride;
+
+            for (size_t x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const double cv = currentRow[x];
+                const double cxx = (currentRow[x + 1] + currentRow[x - 1] - 2.0 * cv) / (dx * dx);
+                const double cyy = (nextRow[x] + previousRow[x] - 2.0 * cv) / (dy * dy);
+                const double czz = (nextZRow[x] + previousZRow[x] - 2.0 * cv) / (dz * dz);
+                outputRow[x] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB - 2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                               3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);
             }
         }
     }
 }
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,

-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+void cahnHilliardUpdateRange(double* CH_RESTRICT const cnew, const double* CH_RESTRICT const cold,
+                             const double* CH_RESTRICT const mu, const size_t firstZ,
+                             const size_t lastZ, const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                             const size_t rowStride, const size_t planeStride, const double D,
+                             const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
+    for (size_t z = firstZ; z <= lastZ; ++z) {
+        const double* const previousPlane = mu + (z - 1) * planeStride;
+        const double* const currentPlane = mu + z * planeStride;
+        const double* const nextPlane = mu + (z + 1) * planeStride;
+        const double* const oldPlane = cold + z * planeStride;
+        double* const newPlane = cnew + z * planeStride;
+
+        for (size_t y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            const double* const previousRow = currentPlane + (y - 1) * rowStride;
+            const double* const currentRow = currentPlane + y * rowStride;
+            const double* const nextRow = currentPlane + (y + 1) * rowStride;
+            const double* const oldRow = oldPlane + y * rowStride;
+            double* const newRow = newPlane + y * rowStride;
+            const double* const previousZRow = previousPlane + y * rowStride;
+            const double* const nextZRow = nextPlane + y * rowStride;
+
+            for (size_t x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const double muv = currentRow[x];
+                const double mxx = (currentRow[x + 1] + currentRow[x - 1] - 2.0 * muv) / (dx * dx);
+                const double myy = (nextRow[x] + previousRow[x] - 2.0 * muv) / (dy * dy);
+                const double mzz = (nextZRow[x] + previousZRow[x] - 2.0 * muv) / (dz * dz);
+                newRow[x] = oldRow[x] + dt * D * (mxx + myy + mzz);
             }
         }
     }
 }

Implemented MPI distributed-memory parallelism.

- Z-slab decomposition with nonblocking halo exchanges overlapped with interior stencil computation.
- Branch-free X/Y stencil halos and optimized local storage.
- Root-only output, distributed validation, and gathered `-r` results preserve serial semantics.
- MPI is now required in [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/cahn-hilliard_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_mpi_r3/cahn-hilliard/CMakeLists.txt:7); executable name remains `cahn_hilliard`.
- Main implementation: [cahn_hilliard.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/cahn-hilliard_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_mpi_r3/cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp:44).

Validated a Release build and exact `-r` output agreement between 1, 2, 3, and excess-rank MPI runs, including single-cell/thin-domain cases.
